- **common-sense**: Apply common-sense judgment as supreme authority before any decision. Trigger when user invokes `/common-sense`, mentions "common sense", "make sense", "practical", "is this sensible", "real world", "would a normal person", or whenever weighing options. ALSO auto-apply silently before any production change, deploy, recommendation, alert, message, purchase, design choice, or any consequential action. ([cfilipemt/common-sense-skill](https://github.com/cfilipemt/common-sense-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
- **common-sense**: Give the agent the judgment a competent engineer already has. Use on every coding task, before commits and PRs, and whenever you are about to invent an API, claim an unrun test, add a file, ask the user which of three identical options they prefer, or write a README section that exists because READMEs usually have one. Also use when the user says common sense, /common-sense, just do it, don't overthink, that's overkill, why did you do that, stop asking, or you are stuck in a retry loop. ([teddytennant/common-sense](https://github.com/teddytennant/common-sense/tree/HEAD/plugins/common-sense/skills/common-sense) · 2 copies)
